### HfStratagemSettings

`StratagemDefinition`: 400-byte rows found by id, `api->stratagem_row`. a stratagem: text, input, uses, cooldown, delivery, payload, packages and traits.

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `type` | +0x00 | u32 | mission? | its StratagemType (must stay) |
| `stable_id` | +0x04 | u32 | mission? | its stable id, which saved loadouts keep (must stay) |
| `name_text` | +0x28 | u32 | mission? | text id of its name (upper case) |
| `cased_name_text` | +0x2C | u32 | mission? | text id of its name |
| `description_text` | +0x30 | u32 | mission? | text id of its description |
| `delivery` | +0x3C | u32 | mission? | how it arrives: 0 Eagle, 1 orbital strike, 2 hellpod, 3 Pelican, 4 mission, 5 barrage, 6 SEAF squad, 7 rearm |
| `code` | +0x40 | u64 | mission? | pointer to its input's directions (u32: 1 up, 2 right, 3 down, 4 left) |
| `code_length` | +0x48 | u32 | mission? | how many directions |
| `uses` | +0x50 | u32 | mission? | uses per mission (0xFFFFFFFF unlimited) |
| `call_in_time` | +0x54 | f32 | now | seconds from the throw to the arrival (unverified) |
| `free_spot_radius` | +0x58 | f32 | mission? | radius searched for a free landing spot (unverified) |
| `linger` | +0x60 | f32 | mission? | seconds it lingers after activating (unverified) |
| `cooldown` | +0x68 | f32 | now | seconds before it can be called again |
| `origin_type` | +0x70 | u32 | mission? | where it comes from (StratagemOriginType) (unverified) |
| `call_in_type` | +0x74 | u32 | mission? | throw, exact location, LZ beacon or hellpad (StratagemCallInType) (unverified) |
| `cooldown_type` | +0x94 | u32 | mission? | individual, shared by the team or by the clan (StratagemCooldownType) (unverified) |
| `payload` | +0x98 | u64 | mission? | pointer to the entities it spawns (u64 resources) |
| `payload_count` | +0xA0 | u32 | mission? | how many |
| `package` | +0xA8 | u64 | mission? | the package it loads for the mission |
| `icon` | +0xB0 | u64 | mission? | its icon resource |
| `hud_type` | +0xB8 | u32 | mission? | loadout section and ping colour: 0 offensive, 1 mission, 2 supply, 3 defensive, 4 special |
| `loadout_weight` | +0xBC | u32 | mission? | loadout slot weight (a loadout keeps at most 4) (unverified) |
| `linked_type` | +0xC8 | u32 | mission? | a linked StratagemType (an Eagle's: Eagle Rearm) (unverified) |
| `beam` | +0xD4 | u32 | mission? | beacon beam colour: 1 red, 2 blue, 3 yellow |
| `loads_item` | +0xF8 | u32 | mission? | a loadout item whose package loads too |
| `traits` | +0x104 | u32 | mission? | its traits (HF_TRAIT_...) |
| `waves` | +0x12C | u32 | mission? | waves minus one (unverified) |
| `beacon_flags` | +0x170 | u8 | mission? | how its thrown beacon lands; bit 1 (2): it sticks only where navmesh is within 0.1 m of the hit and otherwise bounces and is steered toward navmesh (hellpods, vehicles, SEAF; orbitals and Eagles have it clear and stick anywhere); bit 0 not known |

### HfExplosionSettings

`ExplosionSettings`: 152-byte rows found by id. an explosion type.

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `damage` | +0x04 | u32 | now? | the damage id it deals (DamageInfo) (a damage reference) |
| `inner_radius` | +0x10 | f32 | now? | Explosion inner radius (meters) (unverified) |
| `outer_radius` | +0x14 | f32 | now? | Explosion outer radius (meters) (unverified) |
| `shockwave_radius` | +0x18 | f32 | now? | Explosion shockwave radius (meters) (unverified) |
| `shrapnel_count` | +0x50 | u32 | now? | Shrapnel / bomblet count (projectiles) (unverified) |

### HfArcSettings

`ArcSettings`: 104-byte rows found by id. an arc type.

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `velocity` | +0x04 | f32 | now? | Arc velocity (unverified) |
| `range` | +0x08 | f32 | now? | Arc range (meters) (unverified) |
| `distance_at_max_spread` | +0x0C | f32 | now? | Arc distance at maximum spread (meters) (unverified) |
| `distance_at_max_spread_first_shot` | +0x10 | f32 | now? | First-shot distance at maximum spread (meters) (unverified) |
| `max_angle_spread` | +0x14 | f32 | now? | Arc maximum angle spread (degrees) (unverified) |
| `max_angle_spread_first_shot` | +0x18 | f32 | now? | First-shot maximum angle spread (degrees) (unverified) |
| `chain_count` | +0x1C | u32 | now? | Arc maximum chain length (targets) (unverified) |
| `max_split` | +0x20 | u32 | now? | Arc maximum split (targets) (unverified) |
| `damage` | +0x24 | u32 | now? | the damage id it deals (DamageInfo) (a damage reference) |

### HfBeamSettings

`BeamSettings`: 112-byte rows found by id. a beam type.

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `radius` | +0x04 | f32 | now? | Beam radius (meters) (unverified) |
| `length` | +0x08 | f32 | now? | Beam length (meters) (unverified) |
| `damage` | +0x0C | u32 | now? | the damage id it deals (DamageInfo) (a damage reference) |

### HfStatusEffectSettings

`StatusEffectSettings`: 152-byte rows found by id. a status effect.

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `strength` | +0x24 | f32 | now? | Status strength (factor) (unverified) |
| `duration` | +0x28 | f32 | now? | Status duration (seconds) (unverified) |

### HfBoosterSettings

`BoosterSettings`: 56-byte rows found by id. a booster definition (game.dll +0x32FDA90, a row by the booster's value: HF_BOOSTER_...).

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `granted_stratagem` | +0x04 | u32 | now? | The stratagem type the booster grants (15 for Surplus EAT Allocation, else 0) (unverified) |
| `tuning` | +0x08 | f32 | now? | The booster's tuning value, read where its effect applies (what it is depends on the booster: mappings/boosters.json) (unverified) |

### HfPassiveModifier

`PassiveModifier`: 16-byte rows found by id. an armor passive's bonus (id: passive * 16 + index; shared by every wearer).

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | +0x00 | u32 | now? | which bonus it is (must stay) |
| `value` | +0x08 | f32 | now? | the bonus |

### HfPassiveStat

`PassiveStat`: 12-byte rows found by id. an armor passive's stat change (id: passive * 16 + index; applied at spawn from the worn kit).

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `stat` | +0x00 | u32 | respawn? | which stat (must stay) |
| `add` | +0x04 | f32 | respawn? | added to the stat |
| `mul` | +0x08 | f32 | respawn? | the stat multiplied by |

### HfMeleeHitVolume

`MeleeHitVolume`: 248-byte rows found by id. a melee hit volume (static in game.dll, a record by its RVA; HD2Runtime melee_authoring).

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `volume_x` | +0x28 | f32 | now? | Hit volume X (unverified) |
| `volume_y` | +0x2C | f32 | now? | Hit volume Y (unverified) |
| `volume_z` | +0x30 | f32 | now? | Hit volume Z (unverified) |
| `offset_x` | +0x38 | f32 | now? | Hit volume local offset X (unverified) |
| `offset_y` | +0x3C | f32 | now? | Hit volume local offset Y (unverified) |
| `offset_z` | +0x40 | f32 | now? | Hit volume local offset Z (unverified) |
| `repeat_hit_interval` | +0xAC | f32 | now? | Repeat-hit interval (0 once per attack) (unverified) |

### HfEnemyRoster

`EnemyRoster`: 40-byte rows found by id. an enemy roster row's spawn weights (a row by (roster << 16) | row; the weights wherever level 11 put them).

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `weights` | +0x00 | f32[10] | mission? | spawn weight at tech levels (difficulties) 1 to 10 (unverified) |

### HfEnemyRosterLevel11

`EnemyRosterLevel11`: 4-byte rows found by id. an enemy roster row's spawn weight at level 11 (only with a mod that adds it).

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `weight` | +0x00 | f32 | mission? | spawn weight at level 11 (unverified) |

### HfDemolitionTemplate

`DemolitionTemplate`: 16-byte rows found by id. a demolition template (a row by its index, 1 to 9): the force a destruction stage that names it requires, shared by every such stage.

| Field | Offset | Type | Applies | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `index` | +0x00 | u32 | now? | its index (not edited) (unverified) |
| `force` | +0x04 | u32 | now? | the force a stage naming it requires, 1 to 255 (the incoming force is narrowed to a byte) (unverified) |

## Content

Vanilla content constants (`hellforge_content.h`), one list per kind. `hf->find(HF_KIND_..., "name")` resolves the
same names at run time. Each list is folded: open it to see every constant with its value and the game's name for
it.

See also: [Content](modding/content.md) for finding content, including content other mods added.

### HF_ITEM_... (168, `HF_KIND_ITEM`)

Loadout ids of vanilla items, for HfWeapon.base, sound_from and HfStratagem.loads_item. Every session with a weapon added lists the whole arsenal (loadout id, armory type, entity, designation, name) in its log folder as arsenal.tsv.
